{"type":"item.completed","item":{"id":"item_0","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc \"sed -n '1,260p' <TMPDIR>/plan.txt\"","aggregated_output":"## Implementation Plan\n\nFix two bugs reported in issue #6577: (1) false-positive policy-rejection kills when `aggregated_output` of completed Codex commands contains historical larch-log phrases; (2) attempt counters rendered as retries in execution-issue entries.\n\n### Approach\n\n**Primary fix**: `_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt` raw-scans the last 32 KB of the Codex events stream (JSON lines from `codex exec --json`). Completed-command events include `aggregated_output` that can contain phrases like \"blocked by policy\" or \"Rejected(\" from grep output over committed larch-logs, causing a false kill. Fix: strip `aggregated_output` values from JSON-parseable lines before regex scanning. Non-JSON lines (startup noise, test fixtures) pass through unchanged.\n\n**Secondary fix**: `run_logs.py` prints \"auth-retries=1, transient-retries=1\" for policy-rejection entries because `_review_launcher.py` starts both attempt counters at 1 (for the initial run) and always passes them. Subtracting 1 in the \"both counters present\" rendering branch correctly shows 0 retries for single-attempt runs and N-1 for N attempts.\n\n### Edge cases\n\n- Non-JSON lines in the events file pass through unchanged, preserving backward compat with test fixtures and Codex startup messages.\n- If `aggregated_output` is the only content in a JSON line, the stripped version has no trigger-phrase match, so no kill fires.\n- Subtracting 1 only affects the `if retry_count and transient_retry_count:` branch (review-launcher path); the `elif retry_count:` branch (ci-launcher path, 0-based counts) is unchanged.\n\n### Failure modes\n\n1. `json.loads` raises `ValueError` on malformed-but-non-`JSONDecodeError` input: the except clause catches only `json.JSONDecodeError`; other exceptions propagate. Mitigation: Codex events are well-formed; this path is unreachable in practice.\n2. Both counters become 0 after subtraction (policy rejection case): the suffix is suppressed entirely, which is the desired behavior.\n\n### Testing strategy\n\n- Update `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_rejection_fast_fails` (test_agents.py line ~967): ensure the process-kill behavior is preserved. The test's plain-text output (non-JSON) still matches after the fix since non-JSON lines pass through.\n- Add new test `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_no_false_positive_aggregated_output`: write a JSON events file whose `aggregated_output` contains \"exec_command failed\" and \"Rejected(blocked by policy)\" — assert no kill fires and `FAILURE_CLASS=policy-rejection` absent from diag.\n- Update `test_codex_transient_exhaustion_logs_to_implement_tmpdir` (test_launch_review.py line ~1932): change `\"transient-retries=5\"` to `\"transient-retries=4\"` (4 actual retries after subtracting the initial attempt).\n\n### Files to modify/create\n\n### UPDATED: python/larch/agents/_run_external.py\n- Add `_strip_aggregated_output_from_json_lines(text: str) -> str` helper (after the `_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt_bytes` constants block). It iterates lines, parses each as JSON, removes the `aggregated_output` key from dict objects, and returns the modified line; non-JSON lines pass through.\n- In `_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt`, call `_strip_aggregated_output_from_json_lines(bounded)` to produce `scanned`, then replace all three uses of `bounded` in the regex/excerpt logic with `scanned` (both regex guards, the `splitlines()` pass, and the fallback slice).\n\n### UPDATED: python/larch/report/run_logs.py\n- In `append_failure_main`, replace lines 654–657 with logic that subtracts 1 from both `retry_count` and `transient_retry_count` when both are present, and omits the suffix when both adjusted values are 0.\n\n### UPDATED: python/tests/agents/test_agents.py\n- Add `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_no_false_positive_aggregated_output`: write a JSON-line events file via a helper process, verify no kill and no `FAILURE_CLASS=policy-rejection` in diag.\n\n### UPDATED: python/tests/agents/test_launch_review.py\n- In `test_codex_transient_exhaustion_logs_to_implement_tmpdir`, change `\"transient-retries=5\"` to `\"transient-retries=4\"`.\n\ndifficulty: TRIVIAL\ndiff_lines: 65\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}

{"type":"item.completed","item":{"id":"item_0","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -c \"sed -n '1,260p' <TMPDIR>/plan.txt\"","aggregated_output":"## Implementation Plan\n\nFix two bugs reported in issue #6577: (1) false-positive policy-rejection kills when `aggregated_output` of completed Codex commands contains historical larch-log phrases; (2) attempt counters rendered as retries in execution-issue entries.\n\n### Approach\n\n**Primary fix**: `_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt` raw-scans the last 32 KB of the Codex events stream (JSON lines from `codex exec --json`). Completed-command events include `aggregated_output` that can contain phrases like \"blocked by policy\" or \"Rejected(\" from grep output over committed larch-logs, causing a false kill. Fix: strip `aggregated_output` values from JSON-parseable lines before regex scanning. Non-JSON lines (startup noise, test fixtures) pass through unchanged.\n\n**Secondary fix**: `run_logs.py` prints \"auth-retries=1, transient-retries=1\" for policy-rejection entries because `_review_launcher.py` starts both attempt counters at 1 (for the initial run) and always passes them. Subtracting 1 in the \"both counters present\" rendering branch correctly shows 0 retries for single-attempt runs and N-1 for N attempts.\n\n### Edge cases\n\n- Non-JSON lines in the events file pass through unchanged, preserving backward compat with test fixtures and Codex startup messages.\n- If `aggregated_output` is the only content in a JSON line, the stripped version has no trigger-phrase match, so no kill fires.\n- Subtracting 1 only affects the `if retry_count and transient_retry_count:` branch (review-launcher path); the `elif retry_count:` branch (ci-launcher path, 0-based counts) is unchanged.\n\n### Failure modes\n\n1. `json.loads` raises `ValueError` on malformed-but-non-`JSONDecodeError` input: the except clause catches only `json.JSONDecodeError`; other exceptions propagate. Mitigation: Codex events are well-formed; this path is unreachable in practice.\n2. Both count
Failed with exit code 1. Output size: 0 bytes.

Completed-command events include `aggregated_output` that can contain phrases like \"blocked by policy\" or \"Rejected(\" from grep output over committed larch-logs, causing a false kill. Fix: strip `aggregated_output` values from JSON-parseable lines before regex scanning. Non-JSON lines (startup noise, test fixtures) pass through unchanged.\n\n**Secondary fix**: `run_logs.py` prints \"auth-retries=1, transient-retries=1\" for policy-rejection entries because `_review_launcher.py` starts both attempt counters at 1 (for the initial run) and always passes them. Subtracting 1 in the \"both counters present\" rendering branch correctly shows 0 retries for single-attempt runs and N-1 for N attempts.\n\n### Edge cases\n\n- Non-JSON lines in the events file pass through unchanged, preserving backward compat with test fixtures and Codex startup messages.\n- If `aggregated_output` is the only content in a JSON line, the stripped version has no trigger-phrase match, so no kill fires.\n- Subtracting 1 only affects the `if retry_count and transient_retry_count:` branch (review-launcher path); the `elif retry_count:` branch (ci-launcher path, 0-based counts) is unchanged.\n\n### Failure modes\n\n1. `json.loads` raises `ValueError` on malformed-but-non-`JSONDecodeError` input: the except clause catches only `json.JSONDecodeError`; other exceptions propagate. Mitigation: Codex events are well-formed; this path is unreachable in practice.\n2. Both counters become 0 after subtraction (policy rejection case): the suffix is suppressed entirely, which is the desired behavior.\n\n### Testing strategy\n\n- Update `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_rejection_fast_fails` (test_agents.py line ~967): ensure the process-kill behavior is preserved. The test's plain-text output (non-JSON) still matches after the fix since non-JSON lines pass through.\n- Add new test `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_no_false_positive_aggregated_output`: write a JSON events file whose `aggregated_output` contains \"exec_command failed\" and \"Rejected(blocked by policy)\" — assert no kill fires and `FAILURE_CLASS=policy-rejection` absent from diag.\n- Update `test_codex_transient_exhaustion_logs_to_implement_tmpdir` (test_launch_review.py line ~1932): change `\"transient-retries=5\"` to `\"transient-retries=4\"` (4 actual retries after subtracting the initial attempt).\n\n### Files to modify/create\n\n### UPDATED: python/larch/agents/_run_external.py\n- Add `_strip_aggregated_output_from_json_lines(text: str) -> str` helper (after the `_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt_bytes` constants block). It iterates lines, parses each as JSON, removes the `aggregated_output` key from dict objects, and returns the modified line; non-JSON lines pass through.\n- In `_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt`, call `_strip_aggregated_output_from_json_lines(bounded)` to produce `scanned`, then replace all three uses of `bounded` in the regex/excerpt logic with `scanned` (both regex guards, the `splitlines()` pass, and the fallback slice).\n\n### UPDATED: python/larch/report/run_logs.py\n- In `append_failure_main`, replace lines 654–657 with logic that subtracts 1 from both `retry_count` and `transient_retry_count` when both are present, and omits the suffix when both adjusted values are 0.\n\n### UPDATED: python/tests/agents/test_agents.py\n- Add `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_no_false_positive_aggregated_output`: write a JSON-line events file via a helper process, verify no kill and no `FAILURE_CLASS=policy-rejection` in diag.\n\n### UPDATED: python/tests/agents/test_launch_review.py\n- In `test_codex_transient_exhaustion_logs_to_implement_tmpdir`, change `\"transient-retries=5\"` to `\"transient-retries=4\"`.\n\ndifficulty: TRIVIAL\ndiff_lines: 65\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}

{"type":"item.completed","item":{"id":"item_0","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -c \"sed -n '1,260p' <TMPDIR>/plan.txt\"","aggregated_output":"## Implementation Plan\n\nFix two bugs reported in issue #6577: (1) false-positive policy-rejection kills when `aggregated_output` of completed Codex commands contains historical larch-log phrases; (2) attempt counters rendered as retries in execution-issue entries.\n\n### Approach\n\n**Primary fix**: `_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt` raw-scans the last 32 KB of the Codex events stream (JSON lines from `codex exec --json`). Completed-command events include `aggregated_output` that can contain phrases like \"blocked by policy\" or \"Rejected(\" from grep output over committed larch-logs, causing a false kill. Fix: strip `aggregated_output` values from JSON-parseable lines before regex scanning. Non-JSON lines (startup noise, test fixtures) pass through unchanged.\n\n**Secondary fix**: `run_logs.py` prints \"auth-retries=1, transient-retries=1\" for policy-rejection entries because `_review_launcher.py` starts both attempt counters at 1 (for the initial run) and always passes them. Subtracting 1 in the \"both counters present\" rendering branch correctly shows 0 retries for single-attempt runs and N-1 for N attempts.\n\n### Edge cases\n\n- Non-JSON lines in the events file pass through unchanged, preserving backward compat with test fixtures and Codex startup messages.\n- If `aggregated_output` is the only content in a JSON line, the stripped version has no trigger-phrase match, so no kill fires.\n- Subtracting 1 only affects the `if retry_count and transient_retry_count:` branch (review-launcher path); the `elif retry_count:` branch (ci-launcher path, 0-based counts) is unchanged.\n\n### Failure modes\n\n1. `json.loads` raises `ValueError` on malformed-but-non-`JSONDecodeError` input: the except clause catches only `json.JSONDecodeError`; other exceptions propagate. Mitigation: Codex events are well-formed; this path is unreachable in practice.\n2. Both count

{"type":"item.started","item":{"id":"item_0","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc \"sed -n '1,260p' <TMPDIR>/plan.txt\"","aggregated_output":"","exit_code":null,"status":"in_progress"}}
{"type":"item.completed","item":{"id":"item_0","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc \"sed -n '1,260p' <TMPDIR>/plan.txt\"","aggregated_output":"## Implementation Plan\n\nFix two bugs reported in issue #6577: (1) false-positive policy-rejection kills when `aggregated_output` of completed Codex commands contains historical larch-log phrases; (2) attempt counters rendered as retries in execution-issue entries.\n\n### Approach\n\n**Primary fix**: `_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt` raw-scans the last 32 KB of the Codex events stream (JSON lines from `codex exec --json`). Completed-command events include `aggregated_output` that can contain phrases like \"blocked by policy\" or \"Rejected(\" from grep output over committed larch-logs, causing a false kill. Fix: strip `aggregated_output` values from JSON-parseable lines before regex scanning. Non-JSON lines (startup noise, test fixtures) pass through unchanged.\n\n**Secondary fix**: `run_logs.py` prints \"auth-retries=1, transient-retries=1\" for policy-rejection entries because `_review_launcher.py` starts both attempt counters at 1 (for the initial run) and always passes them. Subtracting 1 in the \"both counters present\" rendering branch correctly shows 0 retries for single-attempt runs and N-1 for N attempts.\n\n### Edge cases\n\n- Non-JSON lines in the events file pass through unchanged, preserving backward compat with test fixtures and Codex startup messages.\n- If `aggregated_output` is the only content in a JSON line, the stripped version has no trigger-phrase match, so no kill fires.\n- Subtracting 1 only affects the `if retry_count and transient_retry_count:` branch (review-launcher path); the `elif retry_count:` branch (ci-launcher path, 0-based counts) is unchanged.\n\n### Failure modes\n\n1. `json.loads` raises `ValueError` on malformed-but-non-`JSONDecodeError` input: the except clause catches only `json.JSONDecodeError`; other exceptions propagate. Mitigation: Codex events are well-formed; this path is unreachable in practice.\n2. Both counters become 0 after subtraction (policy rejection case): the suffix is suppressed entirely, which is the desired behavior.\n\n### Testing strategy\n\n- Update `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_rejection_fast_fails` (test_agents.py line ~967): ensure the process-kill behavior is preserved. The test's plain-text output (non-JSON) still matches after the fix since non-JSON lines pass through.\n- Add new test `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_no_false_positive_aggregated_output`: write a JSON events file whose `aggregated_output` contains \"exec_command failed\" and \"Rejected(blocked by policy)\" — assert no kill fires and `FAILURE_CLASS=policy-rejection` absent from diag.\n- Update `test_codex_transient_exhaustion_logs_to_implement_tmpdir` (test_launch_review.py line ~1932): change `\"transient-retries=5\"` to `\"transient-retries=4\"` (4 actual retries after subtracting the initial attempt).\n\n### Files to modify/create\n\n### UPDATED: python/larch/agents/_run_external.py\n- Add `_strip_aggregated_output_from_json_lines(text: str) -> str` helper (after the `_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt_bytes` constants block). It iterates lines, parses each as JSON, removes the `aggregated_output` key from dict objects, and returns the modified line; non-JSON lines pass through.\n- In `_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt`, call `_strip_aggregated_output_from_json_lines(bounded)` to produce `scanned`, then replace all three uses of `bounded` in the regex/excerpt logic with `scanned` (both regex guards, the `splitlines()` pass, and the fallback slice).\n\n### UPDATED: python/larch/report/run_logs.py\n- In `append_failure_main`, replace lines 654–657 with logic that subtracts 1 from both `retry_count` and `transient_retry_count` when both are present, and omits the suffix when both adjusted values are 0.\n\n### UPDATED: python/tests/agents/test_agents.py\n- Add `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_no_false_positive_aggregated_output`: write a JSON-line events file via a helper process, verify no kill and no `FAILURE_CLASS=policy-rejection` in diag.\n\n### UPDATED: python/tests/agents/test_launch_review.py\n- In `test_codex_transient_exhaustion_logs_to_implement_tmpdir`, change `\"transient-retries=5\"` to `\"transient-retries=4\"`.\n\ndifficulty: TRIVIAL\ndiff_lines: 65\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}

Production events nest command output under `item.aggregated_output` in `item.completed` / `command_execution` rows (see `larch-logs/design/693522DE/execution-issues.md`). A flat top-level key strip misses the real false-positive path; stripping every `aggregated_output` would hide genuine rejections in failed commands.\n\nAdd `_sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan(text: str) -> str` (after the `_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt_bytes` constants block):\n\n1. Iterate lines of the bounded tail text.\n2. Non-JSON lines pass through unchanged (startup noise, legacy plain-text fixtures).\n3. JSON lines: `json.loads` each line; on `JSONDecodeError`, pass the original line through.\n4. Walk the parsed value recursively. For every dict that owns an `aggregated_output` key, gate removal on that dict's `exit_code`:\n   - **Strip** `aggregated_output` when `exit_code == 0` (successful completion whose stdout/stderr body can quote grep hits over committed larch-logs).\n   - **Strip** when `exit_code is None` and `aggregated_output` is empty (in-progress rows; no-op but safe).\n   - **Preserve** `aggregated_output` when `exit_code` is any other non-zero integer (failed command evidence must remain scannable).\n   - Recursion covers nested `item` dicts and any other dict nodes that carry both fields; do not assume a flat envelope.\n5. Re-serialize sanitized JSON objects with `json.dumps(..., ensure_ascii=False)` plus the original line terminator.\n\nIn `_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt`, call `_sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan(bounded)` to produce `scanned`, then use `scanned` everywhere `bounded` is used today (both regex guards, the `splitlines()` excerpt pass, and the fallback slice). Real policy rejections still surface via non-zero `exit_code` rows, error events, and plain-text startup/diag lines outside successful completions.\n\n**Secondary fix — retry suffix rendering**\n\n`_review_launcher.py` passes `auth_attempt` and `transient_attempt` starting at 1 (initial run). In `append_failure_main`, when both `--retry-count` and `--transient-retry-count` are present, subtract 1 from each before rendering; omit the retry suffix entirely when both adjusted values are 0 (single-attempt policy rejection). Leave the `elif args.retry_count:` ci-launcher branch unchanged (0-based counts).\n\n### Edge cases\n\n- Nested `item.completed` with `exit_code: 0` and trigger phrases only inside `item.aggregated_output` must not kill (primary regression target).\n- Nested or flat command row with non-zero `exit_code` and policy phrases in `aggregated_output` must still match after sanitization (preserves fast-kill for genuine failures).\n- `item.started` rows (`exit_code: null`, empty `aggregated_output`) pass through; stripping is a no-op.\n- Malformed JSON lines pass through unchanged; existing plain-text policy-rejection tests keep working.\n- Both adjusted retry counters at 0 suppress the suffix (desired for policy rejection with no retries).\n\n### Failure modes\n\n1. **`exit_code` absent on a dict with `aggregated_output`**: treat as preserve (do not strip) so ambiguous rows cannot hide real failures.\n2. **Deeply nested trees**: recursive walk must visit all dict nodes; shallow `item` pop alone is insufficient if Codex adds other nesting later.\n3. **Re-serialization changes key order**: irrelevant; regex scan is order-independent.\n\n### Testing strategy\n\n- Keep `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_rejection_fast_fails` unchanged (plain-text events still kill).\n- Keep `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_rejection_requires_both_families` unchanged.\n- Add `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_no_false_positive_aggregated_output`:\n  - Pre-write a realistic JSONL events file to `paths.events` before launch, shaped like production Codex output:\n    `{\"type\":\"item.completed\",\"item\":{\"id\":\"item_0\",\"type\":\"command_execution\",\"command\":\"/bin/bash -c \\\"rg ... larch-logs\\\"\",\"aggregated_output\":\"... exec_command failed ... Rejected(blocked by policy) ...\",\"exit_code\":0,\"status\":\"completed\"}}`\n  - Run a long-sleep stub child so the policy watcher has time to scan the events file.\n  - Assert the process is not killed early, diag lacks `FAILURE_CLASS=policy-rejection` and `POLICY_REJECTION=true`.\n- Add `test_sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan_preserves_failed_command_output` (unit-level, same module or test_agents):\n  - Feed a JSONL line with `exit_code: 1` and trigger phrases inside nested `item.aggregated_output`; assert sanitized text still contains the phrases.\n- Update `test_codex_transient_exhaustion_logs_to_implement_tmpdir`: change `\"transient-retries=5\"` to `\"transient-retries=4\"` (four retries after subtracting the initial attempt).\n\n### Files to modify/create\n\n### UPDATED: python/larch/agents/_run_external.py\n\n- Add `_should_strip_aggregated_output(exit_code: object) -> bool` returning true only for `exit_code == 0` or (`exit_code is None` and caller confirms empty output).\n- Add `_strip_gated_aggregated_output(node: object) -> None` recursive in-place walker: for each dict with `aggregated_output`, pop the key when `_should_strip_aggregated_output(node.get(\"exit_code\"))` is true; Production events nest command output under `item.aggregated_output` in `item.completed` / `command_execution` rows (see `larch-logs/design/693522DE/execution-issues.md`). A flat top-level key strip misses the real false-positive path; stripping every `aggregated_output` would hide genuine rejections in failed commands.\n\nAdd `_sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan(text: str) -> str` (after the `_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt_bytes` constants block):\n\n1. Iterate lines of the bounded tail text.\n2. Non-JSON lines pass through unchanged (startup noise, legacy plain-text fixtures).\n3. JSON lines: `json.loads` each line; on `JSONDecodeError`, pass the original line through.\n4. Walk the parsed value recursively. For every dict that owns an `aggregated_output` key, gate removal on that dict's `exit_code`:\n   - **Strip** `aggregated_output` when `exit_code == 0` (successful completion whose stdout/stderr body can quote grep hits over committed larch-logs).\n   - **Strip** when `exit_code is None` and `aggregated_output` is empty (in-progress rows; no-op but safe).\n   - **Preserve** `aggregated_output` when `exit_code` is any other non-zero integer (failed command evidence must remain scannable).\n   - Recursion covers nested `item` dicts and any other dict nodes that carry both fields; do not assume a flat envelope.\n5. Production events nest command output under `item.aggregated_output` in `item.completed` / `command_execution` rows (see `larch-logs/design/693522DE/execution-issues.md`). A flat top-level key strip misses the real false-positive path; stripping every `aggregated_output` would hide genuine rejections in failed commands.\n\nAdd `_sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan(text: str) -> str` (after the `_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt_bytes` constants block):\n\n1. Iterate lines of the bounded tail text.\n2. Non-JSON lines pass through unchanged (startup noise, legacy plain-text fixtures).\n3. JSON lines: `json.loads` each line; on `JSONDecodeError`, pass the original line through.\n4. Walk the parsed value recursively. For every dict that owns an `aggregated_output` key, gate removal on that dict's `exit_code`:\n   - **Strip** `aggregated_output` when `exit_code == 0` (successful completion whose stdout/stderr body can quote grep hits over committed larch-logs).\n   - **Strip** when `exit_code is None` and `aggregated_output` is empty (in-progress rows; no-op but safe).\n   - **Preserve** `aggregated_output` when `exit_code` is any other non-zero integer (failed command evidence must remain scannable).\n   - Recursion covers nested `item` dicts and any other dict nodes that carry both fields; do not assume a flat envelope.\n5. Re-serialize sanitized JSON objects with `json.dumps(..., ensure_ascii=False)` plus the original line terminator.\n\nIn `_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt`, call `_sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan(bounded)` to produce `scanned`, then use `scanned` everywhere `bounded` is used today (both regex guards, the `splitlines()` excerpt pass, and the fallback slice). Real policy rejections still surface via non-zero `exit_code` rows, error events, and plain-text startup/diag lines outside successful completions.\n\n**Secondary fix — retry suffix rendering**\n\n`_review_launcher.py` passes `auth_attempt` and `transient_attempt` starting at 1 (initial run). In `append_failure_main`, when both `--retry-count` and `--transient-retry-count` are present, subtract 1 from each before rendering; omit the retry suffix entirely when both adjusted values are 0 (single-attempt policy rejection). Leave the `elif args.retry_count:` ci-launcher branch unchanged (0-based counts).\n\n### Edge cases\n\n- Nested `item.completed` with `exit_code: 0` and trigger phrases only inside `item.aggregated_output` must not kill (primary regression target).\n- Nested or flat command row with non-zero `exit_code` and policy phrases in `aggregated_output` must still match after sanitization (preserves fast-kill for genuine failures).\n- `item.started` rows (`exit_code: null`, empty `aggregated_output`) pass through; stripping is a no-op.\n- Malformed JSON lines pass through unchanged; existing plain-text policy-rejection tests keep working.\n- Both adjusted retry counters at 0 suppress the suffix (desired for policy rejection with no retries).\n\n### Failure modes\n\n1. **`exit_code` absent on a dict with `aggregated_output`**: treat as preserve (do not strip) so ambiguous rows cannot hide real failures.\n2. **Deeply nested trees**: recursive walk must visit all dict nodes; shallow `item` pop alone is insufficient if Codex adds other nesting later.\n3. **Re-serialization changes key order**: irrelevant; regex scan is order-independent.\n\n### Testing strategy\n\n- Keep `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_rejection_fast_fails` unchanged (plain-text events still kill).\n- Keep `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_rejection_requires_both_families` unchanged.\n- Add `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_no_false_positive_aggregated_output`:\n  - Pre-write a realistic JSONL events file to `paths.events` before launch, shaped like production Codex output:\n    `{\"type\":\"item.completed\",\"item\":{\"id\":\"item_0\",\"type\":\"command_execution\",\"command\":\"/bin/bash -c \\\"rg ... larch-logs\\\"\",\"aggregated_output\":\"... exec_command failed ... Rejected(blocked by policy) ...\",\"exit_code\":0,\"status\":\"completed\"}}`\n  - Run a long-sleep stub child so the policy watcher has time to scan the events file.\n  - Assert the process is not killed early, diag lacks `FAILURE_CLASS=policy-rejection` and `POLICY_REJECTION=true`.\n- Add `test_sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan_preserves_failed_command_output` (unit-level, same module or test_agents):\n  - Feed a JSONL line with `exit_code: 1` and trigger phrases inside nested `item.aggregated_output`; assert sanitized text still contains the phrases.\n- Update `test_codex_transient_exhaustion_logs_to_implement_tmpdir`: change `\"transient-retries=5\"` to `\"transient-retries=4\"` (four retries after subtracting the initial attempt).\n\n### Files to modify/create\n\n### UPDATED: python/larch/agents/_run_external.py\n\n- Add `_should_strip_aggregated_output(exit_code: object) -> bool` returning true only for `exit_code == 0` or (`exit_code is None` and caller confirms empty output).\n- Add `_strip_gated_aggregated_output(node: object) -> None` recursive in-place walker: for each dict with `aggregated_output`, pop the key when `_should_strip_aggregated_output(node.get(\"exit_code\"))` is true; recurse into dict values and list elements.\n- Add `_sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan(text: str) -> str`: line-wise JSON parse → `_strip_gated_aggregated_output` → re-serialize; non-JSON lines unchanged.\n- In `_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt`, replace direct use of `bounded` in regex/excerpt logic with `scanned = _sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan(bounded)`.\n\n### UPDATED: python/larch/report/run_logs.py\n\n- In `append_failure_main` (lines 654–657), when both retry counters are present: parse integers, subtract 1 from each, render `auth-retries` / `transient-retries` only when the adjusted value is > 0; {"type":"item.completed","item":{"id":"item_0","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -c 'cat <TMPDIR>/plan.txt'","aggregated_output":"## Plan\n\nFix two bugs from issue #6577: (1) false-positive policy-rejection kills when completed Codex command `aggregated_output` (including nested `item.aggregated_output`) quotes historical larch-log phrases; (2) attempt counters rendered as retries in execution-issue entries.\n\n### Approach\n\n**Primary fix — gated, recursive `aggregated_output` stripping before regex scan**\n\n`_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt` raw-scans the last 32 KB of the Codex events stream (JSONL from `codex exec --json`). Production events nest command output under `item.aggregated_output` in `item.completed` / `command_execution` rows (see `larch-logs/design/693522DE/execution-issues.md`). A flat top-level key strip misses the real false-positive path; stripping every `aggregated_output` would hide genuine rejections in failed commands.\n\nAdd `_sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan(text: str) -> str` (after the `_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt_bytes` constants block):\n\n1. Iterate lines of the bounded tail text.\n2. Non-JSON lines pass through unchanged (startup noise, legacy plain-text fixtures).\n3. JSON lines: `json.loads` each line; on `JSONDecodeError`, pass the original line through.\n4. Walk the parsed value recursively. For every dict that owns an `aggregated_output` key, gate removal on that dict's `exit_code`:\n   - **Strip** `aggregated_output` when `exit_code == 0` (successful completion whose stdout/stderr body can quote grep hits over committed larch-logs).\n   - **Strip** when `exit_code is None` and `aggregated_output` is empty (in-progress rows; no-op but safe).\n   - **Preserve** `aggregated_output` when `exit_code` is any other non-zero integer (failed command evidence must remain scannable).\n   - Recursion covers nested `item` dicts and any other dict nodes that carry both fields; do not assume a flat envelope.\n5. Re-serialize sanitized JSON objects with `json.dumps(..., ensure_ascii=False)` plus the original line terminator.\n\nIn `_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt`, call `_sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan(bounded)` to produce `scanned`, then use `scanned` everywhere `bounded` is used today (both regex guards, the `splitlines()` excerpt pass, and the fallback slice). Real policy rejections still surface via non-zero `exit_code` rows, error events, and plain-text startup/diag lines outside successful completions.\n\n**Secondary fix — retry suffix rendering**\n\n`_review_launcher.py` passes `auth_attempt` and `transient_attempt` starting at 1 (initial run). In `append_failure_main`, when both `--retry-count` and `--transient-retry-count` are present, subtract 1 from each before rendering; omit the retry suffix entirely when both adjusted values are 0 (single-attempt policy rejection). Leave the `elif args.retry_count:` ci-launcher branch unchanged (0-based counts).\n\n### Edge cases\n\n- Nested `item.completed` with `exit_code: 0` and trigger phrases only inside `item.aggregated_output` must not kill (primary regression target).\n- Nested or flat command row with non-zero `exit_code` and policy phrases in `aggregated_output` must still match after sanitization (preserves fast-kill for genuine failures).\n- `item.started` rows (`exit_code: null`, empty `aggregated_output`) pass through; stripping is a no-op.\n- Malformed JSON lines pass through unchanged; existing plain-text policy-rejection tests keep working.\n- Both adjusted retry counters at 0 suppress the suffix (desired for policy rejection with no retries).\n\n### Failure modes\n\n1. **`exit_code` absent on a dict with `aggregated_output`**: treat as preserve (do not strip) so ambiguous rows cannot hide real failures.\n2. **Deeply nested trees**: recursive walk must visit all dict nodes; shallow `item` pop alone is insufficient if Codex adds other nesting later.\n3. **Re-serialization changes key order**: irrelevant; regex scan is order-independent.\n\n### Testing strategy\n\n- Keep `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_rejection_fast_fails` unchanged (plain-text events still kill).\n- Keep `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_rejection_requires_both_families` unchanged.\n- Add `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_no_false_positive_aggregated_output`:\n  - Pre-write a realistic JSONL events file to `paths.events` before launch, shaped like production Codex output:\n    `{\"type\":\"item.completed\",\"item\":{\"id\":\"item_0\",\"type\":\"command_execution\",\"command\":\"/bin/bash -c \\\"rg ... larch-logs\\\"\",\"aggregated_output\":\"... exec_command failed ... Rejected(blocked by policy) ...\",\"exit_code\":0,\"status\":\"completed\"}}`\n  - Run a long-sleep stub child so the policy watcher has time to scan the events file.\n  - Assert the process is not killed early, diag lacks `FAILURE_CLASS=policy-rejection` and `POLICY_REJECTION=true`.\n- Add `test_sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan_preserves_failed_command_output` (unit-level, same module or test_agents):\n  - Feed a JSONL line with `exit_code: 1` and trigger phrases inside nested `item.aggregated_output`; assert sanitized text still contains the phrases.\n- Update `test_codex_transient_exhaustion_logs_to_implement_tmpdir`: change `\"transient-retries=5\"` to `\"transient-retries=4\"` (four retries after subtracting the initial attempt).\n\n### Files to modify/create\n\n### UPDATED: python/larch/agents/_run_external.py\n\n- Add `_should_strip_aggregated_output(exit_code: object) -> bool` returning true only for `exit_code == 0` or (`exit_code is None` and caller confirms empty output).\n- Add `_strip_gated_aggregated_output(node: object) -> None` recursive in-place walker: for each dict with `aggregated_output`, pop the key when `_should_strip_aggregated_output(node.get(\"exit_code\"))` is true; recurse into dict values and list elements.\n- Add `_sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan(text: str) -> str`: line-wise JSON parse → `_strip_gated_aggregated_output` → re-serialize; non-JSON lines unchanged.\n- In `_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt`, replace direct use of `bounded` in regex/excerpt logic with `scanned = _sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan(bounded)`.\n\n### UPDATED: python/larch/report/run_logs.py\n\n- In `append_failure_main` (lines 654–657), when both retry counters are present: parse integers, subtract 1 from each, render `auth-retries` / `transient-retries` only when the adjusted value is > 0; skip the whole retry suffix when both adjusted values are 0.\n\n### UPDATED: python/tests/agents/test_agents.py\n\n- Add `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_no_false_positive_aggregated_output` with nested `item.completed` / `command_execution` fixture (`exit_code: 0`) as above.\n- Add `test_sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan_preserves_failed_command_output` for the `exit_code != 0` preserve path.\n\n### UPDATED: python/tests/agents/test_launch_review.py\n\n- In `test_codex_transient_exhaustion_logs_to_implement_tmpdir`, change expected `\"transient-retries=5\"` to `\"transient-retries=4\"`.\n\ndifficulty: TRIVIAL\ndiff_lines: 95\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}

{"type":"item.completed","item":{"id":"item_0","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc \"sed -n '1,240p' <TMPDIR>/plan.txt\"","aggregated_output":"## Plan\n\nFix two bugs from issue #6577: (1) false-positive policy-rejection kills when completed Codex command `aggregated_output` (including nested `item.aggregated_output`) quotes historical larch-log phrases; (2) attempt counters rendered as retries in execution-issue entries.\n\n### Approach\n\n**Primary fix — gated, recursive `aggregated_output` stripping before regex scan**\n\n`_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt` raw-scans the last 32 KB of the Codex events stream (JSONL from `codex exec --json`). Production events nest command output under `item.aggregated_output` in `item.completed` / `command_execution` rows (see `larch-logs/design/693522DE/execution-issues.md`). A flat top-level key strip misses the real false-positive path; stripping every `aggregated_output` would hide genuine rejections in failed commands.\n\nAdd `_sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan(text: str) -> str` (after the `_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt_bytes` constants block):\n\n1. Iterate lines of the bounded tail text.\n2. Non-JSON lines pass through unchanged (startup noise, legacy plain-text fixtures).\n3. JSON lines: `json.loads` each line; on `JSONDecodeError`, pass the original line through.\n4. Walk the parsed value recursively. For every dict that owns an `aggregated_output` key, gate removal on that dict's `exit_code`:\n   - **Strip** `aggregated_output` when `exit_code == 0` (successful completion whose stdout/stderr body can quote grep hits over committed larch-logs).\n   - **Strip** when `exit_code is None` and `aggregated_output` is empty (in-progress rows; no-op but safe).\n   - **Preserve** `aggregated_output` when `exit_code` is any other non-zero integer (failed command evidence must remain scannable).\n   - Recursion covers nested `item` dicts and any other dict nodes that carry both fields; do not assume a flat envelope.\n5. Re-serialize sanitized JSON objects with `json.dumps(..., ensure_ascii=False)` plus the original line terminator.\n\nIn `_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt`, call `_sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan(bounded)` to produce `scanned`, then use `scanned` everywhere `bounded` is used today (both regex guards, the `splitlines()` excerpt pass, and the fallback slice). Real policy rejections still surface via non-zero `exit_code` rows, error events, and plain-text startup/diag lines outside successful completions.\n\n**Secondary fix — retry suffix rendering**\n\n`_review_launcher.py` passes `auth_attempt` and `transient_attempt` starting at 1 (initial run). In `append_failure_main`, when both `--retry-count` and `--transient-retry-count` are present, subtract 1 from each before rendering; omit the retry suffix entirely when both adjusted values are 0 (single-attempt policy rejection). Leave the `elif args.retry_count:` ci-launcher branch unchanged (0-based counts).\n\n### Edge cases\n\n- Nested `item.completed` with `exit_code: 0` and trigger phrases only inside `item.aggregated_output` must not kill (primary regression target).\n- Nested or flat command row with non-zero `exit_code` and policy phrases in `aggregated_output` must still match after sanitization (preserves fast-kill for genuine failures).\n- `item.started` rows (`exit_code: null`, empty `aggregated_output`) pass through; stripping is a no-op.\n- Malformed JSON lines pass through unchanged; existing plain-text policy-rejection tests keep working.\n- Both adjusted retry counters at 0 suppress the suffix (desired for policy rejection with no retries).\n\n### Failure modes\n\n1. **`exit_code` absent on a dict with `aggregated_output`**: treat as preserve (do not strip) so ambiguous rows cannot hide real failures.\n2. **Deeply nested trees**: recursive walk must visit all dict nodes; shallow `item` pop alone is insufficient if Codex adds other nesting later.\n3. **Re-serialization changes key order**: irrelevant; regex scan is order-independent.\n\n### Testing strategy\n\n- Keep `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_rejection_fast_fails` unchanged (plain-text events still kill).\n- Keep `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_rejection_requires_both_families` unchanged.\n- Add `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_no_false_positive_aggregated_output`:\n  - Pre-write a realistic JSONL events file to `paths.events` before launch, shaped like production Codex output:\n    `{\"type\":\"item.completed\",\"item\":{\"id\":\"item_0\",\"type\":\"command_execution\",\"command\":\"/bin/bash -c \\\"rg ... larch-logs\\\"\",\"aggregated_output\":\"... exec_command failed ... Rejected(blocked by policy) ...\",\"exit_code\":0,\"status\":\"completed\"}}`\n  - Run a long-sleep stub child so the policy watcher has time to scan the events file.\n  - Assert the process is not killed early, diag lacks `FAILURE_CLASS=policy-rejection` and `POLICY_REJECTION=true`.\n- Add `test_sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan_preserves_failed_command_output` (unit-level, same module or test_agents):\n  - Feed a JSONL line with `exit_code: 1` and trigger phrases inside nested `item.aggregated_output`; assert sanitized text still contains the phrases.\n- Update `test_codex_transient_exhaustion_logs_to_implement_tmpdir`: change `\"transient-retries=5\"` to `\"transient-retries=4\"` (four retries after subtracting the initial attempt).\n\n### Files to modify/create\n\n### UPDATED: python/larch/agents/_run_external.py\n\n- Add `_should_strip_aggregated_output(exit_code: object) -> bool` returning true only for `exit_code == 0` or (`exit_code is None` and caller confirms empty output).\n- Add `_strip_gated_aggregated_output(node: object) -> None` recursive in-place walker: for each dict with `aggregated_output`, pop the key when `_should_strip_aggregated_output(node.get(\"exit_code\"))` is true; recurse into dict values and list elements.\n- Add `_sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan(text: str) -> str`: line-wise JSON parse → `_strip_gated_aggregated_output` → re-serialize; non-JSON lines unchanged.\n- In `_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt`, replace direct use of `bounded` in regex/excerpt logic with `scanned = _sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan(bounded)`.\n\n### UPDATED: python/larch/report/run_logs.py\n\n- In `append_failure_main` (lines 654–657), when both retry counters are present: parse integers, subtract 1 from each, render `auth-retries` / `transient-retries` only when the adjusted value is > 0; skip the whole retry suffix when both adjusted values are 0.\n\n### UPDATED: python/tests/agents/test_agents.py\n\n- Add `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_no_false_positive_aggregated_output` with nested `item.completed` / `command_execution` fixture (`exit_code: 0`) as above.\n- Add `test_sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan_preserves_failed_command_output` for the `exit_code != 0` preserve path.\n\n### UPDATED: python/tests/agents/test_launch_review.py\n\n- In `test_codex_transient_exhaustion_logs_to_implement_tmpdir`, change expected `\"transient-retries=5\"` to `\"transient-retries=4\"`.\n\ndifficulty: TRIVIAL\ndiff_lines: 95\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}

Create issue  describing the task/problem with `/issue` or `/bug` or manually\n2. Design it with `/design` (detailed reviewed design gets stored in tracking issue)\n3. Implement it with `/implement`\n\n## Support Skills\n\n- Manage issues and their dependencies: `/issue`, `/bug`, `/combine-issues`, `/block-issue`, `/deps`\n- Various analysis tools: `/report-tokens`, `/fluff-analysis`, `/difficulty-calibration`, `/rejected-analysis`, `/analyze-issues`, `/audit-runs`\n- `larch` management: `/status`, `/upgrade-larch`, `/larch-size`\n\n## Table of Contents\n\n- **Setup**\n  - [Installation and Setup](docs/installation-and-setup.md) — prerequisites, auth (API keys or web login) for Claude / Codex / Cursor, plugin install and permissions, `/status` validation, `/upgrade-larch`\n  - [Preparing Your Repository](docs/preparing-your-repo.md). Ready your repo for larch and agent-assisted development: instruction files (`CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md`), guardrails (guidelines, hooks, linters), and the `checks run-relevant` contract\n  - [Contributing](docs/contributing.md) — local dev plugin install, plugin cache vs. working-tree version, Mermaid CLI setup\n  - [Clean-Main Entry Contract](docs/clean-main-contract.md) — the `/implement` and `/design` clean `main` entry preconditions, plus `/implement` feature-branch continuation\n  - [Fork CI Dry-Runs](docs/forked.md) — remote setup for `/implement --forked`\n  - [macOS Keychain Interactions](docs/macos-keychain-interactions.md) — Cursor `CURSOR_API_KEY` and keychain auth troubleshooting\n  - [Optional Helpers](docs/optional-helpers.md) — installing optional tools like ast-grep\n- **Reference**\n  - [Features](#features)\n  - [Skills](#skills)\n  - [Aliases](#aliases)\n  - [Review Agents](docs/review-agents.md) — the unified `code-reviewer` archetype\n  - [Run Logs](docs/run-logs.md) — committed `larch-logs/` batch files, manifest, and tracking-issue comments\n  - [Topology Projection](docs/topology.md) — stable anchors for cross-doc topology counts\n  - [Linting](docs/linting.md) — linters, Makefile targets, halt-rate regression harness\n  - [Issue-Anchored Plan](docs/issue-anchored-plan.md) — **live** wire format for the /design ↔ /implement plan handoff and clarification round-trip\n- **Architecture and workflow**\n  - [Workflow Lifecycle](docs/workflow-lifecycle.md) — how skills compose end-to-end\n  - [Agent System](docs/agents.md) — parallel subagent orchestration\n  - [Design Flow](docs/collaborative-sketches.md) — direct plan drafting and plan review\n  - [Voting Process](docs/voting-process.md) — the voting panel protocol\n  - [Point Competition](docs/point-competition.md) — reviewer scoring system\n\n## Features\n\n- **[Direct design planning and plan reviews](docs/collaborative-sketches.md)** — Step 2b drafts the plan directly, then the [validation panel](docs/topology.md#design.plan_review.cursor_archetypes) reviews it.\n- **[Voting-based review resolution](docs/voting-process.md)** — The YES/NO panel protocol adjudicates plan and code review findings.\n- **[Reviewer competition scoring](docs/point-competition.md)** — Reviewers earn points based on finding quality; a scoreboard tracks accepted, neutral, and rejected findings.\n- **[Tracked runs](docs/run-logs.md)** — `/implement` writes full run artifacts to committed `larch-logs/` files and keeps the tracking issue slim with marker-keyed summary comments.\n- **Tiered architectural knowledge** — Optional `ARCHITECTURAL_INVARIANTS.md` and `ARCHITECTURAL_GUIDELINES.md` files are supplied to coders and reviewers as untrusted, scope-bound evidence.\n\n## Skills\n\nlarch ships **public skills** with the plugin (`skills/`); **private** skills live under `.claude/skills/` and are dev-only (not exported). Both are listed below; shortcut **aliases** are in the [Aliases](#aliases) section. See [docs/skills.md](docs/skills.md) for full per-skill detail.\n\n### Public skills\n\n<table>\n  <thead>\n    <tr><th>Name</th><th>Arguments</th></tr>\n  </thead>\n  <tbody>\n    <tr>\n      <td><a href=\"docs/skills.md#alias\"><code>/alias</code></a></td>\n      <td><code>[--merge] [--private] &lt;alias-name&gt; &lt;target-skill&gt; [preset-flags...]</code></td>\n    </tr>\n    <tr><td colspan=\"2\">Create an alias for a larch skill with preset flags. Auto-routes to <code>skills/&lt;n&gt;/</code> in plugin source repos and <code>.claude/skills/&lt;n&gt;/</code> elsewhere; <code>--private</code> forces the latter.</td></tr>\n    <tr><td colspan=\"2\"><hr></td></tr>\n    <tr>\n      <td><a href=\"docs/skills.md#block-issue\"><code>/block-issue</code></a></td>\n      <td><code>&lt;ISSUE_A&gt; &lt;ISSUE_B&gt; [--repo owner/name]</code></td>\n    </tr>\n    <tr><td colspan=\"2\">Express a native GitHub blocked-by relationship between two issues using the <code>addBlockedBy</code> GraphQL mutation. Repo auto-detected when <code>--repo</code> is omitted.</td></tr>\n    <tr><td colspan=\"2\"><hr></td></tr>\n    <tr>\n      <td><a href=\"docs/skills.md#bug\"><code>/bug</code></a></td>\n      <td><code>[--urgent] &lt;bug description&gt;</code></td>\n    </tr>\n    <tr><td colspan=\"2\">Investigate

Create issue  describing the task/problem with `/issue` or `/bug` or manually\n2. Design it with `/design` (detailed reviewed design gets stored in tracking issue)\n3. Implement it with `/implement`\n\n## Support Skills\n\n- Manage issues and their dependencies: `/issue`, `/bug`, `/combine-issues`, `/block-issue`, `/deps`\n- Various analysis tools: `/report-tokens`, `/fluff-analysis`, `/difficulty-calibration`, `/rejected-analysis`, `/analyze-issues`, `/audit-runs`\n- `larch` management: `/status`, `/upgrade-larch`, `/larch-size`\n\n## Table of Contents\n\n- **Setup**\n  - [Installation and Setup](docs/installation-and-setup.md) — prerequisites, auth (API keys or web login) for Claude / Codex / Cursor, plugin install and permissions, `/status` validation, `/upgrade-larch`\n  - [Preparing Your Repository](docs/preparing-your-repo.md). Ready your repo for larch and agent-assisted development: instruction files (`CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md`), guardrails (guidelines, hooks, linters), and the `checks run-relevant` contract\n  - [Contributing](docs/contributing.md) — local dev plugin install, plugin cache vs. working-tree version, Mermaid CLI setup\n  - [Clean-Main Entry Contract](docs/clean-main-contract.md) — the `/implement` and `/design` clean `main` entry preconditions, plus `/implement` feature-branch continuation\n  - [Fork CI Dry-Runs](docs/forked.md) — remote setup for `/implement --forked`\n  - [macOS Keychain Interactions](docs/macos-keychain-interactions.md) — Cursor `CURSOR_API_KEY` and keychain auth troubleshooting\n  - [Optional Helpers](docs/optional-helpers.md) — installing optional tools like ast-grep\n- **Reference**\n  - [Topology Projection](docs/topology.md) — stable anchors for cross-doc topology counts\n  - [Linting](docs/linting.md) — linters, Makefile targets, halt-rate regression harness\n  - [Issue-Anchored Plan](docs/issue-anchored-plan.md) — **live** wire format for the /design ↔ /implement plan handoff and clarification round-trip\n- **Architecture and workflow**\n  - [Voting Process](docs/voting-process.md) — the voting panel protocol\n  - [Point Competition](docs/point-competition.md) — reviewer scoring system\n\n## Features\n\n- **[Direct design planning and plan reviews](docs/collaborative-sketches.md)** — Step 2b drafts the plan directly, then the [validation panel](docs/topology.md#design.plan_review.cursor_archetypes) reviews it.\n- **[Voting-based review resolution](docs/voting-process.md)** — The YES/NO panel protocol adjudicates plan and code review findings.\n- **[Reviewer competition scoring](docs/point-competition.md)** — Reviewers earn points based on finding quality; a scoreboard tracks accepted, neutral, and rejected findings.\n- **[Tracked runs](docs/run-logs.md)** — `/implement` writes full run artifacts to committed `larch-logs/` files and keeps the tracking issue slim with marker-keyed summary comments.\n- **Tiered architectural knowledge** — Optional `ARCHITECTURAL_INVARIANTS.md` and `ARCHITECTURAL_GUIDELINES.md` files are supplied to coders and reviewers as untrusted, scope-bound evidence.\n\n## Skills\n\nlarch ships **public skills** with the plugin (`skills/`); **private** skills live under `.claude/skills/` and are dev-only (not exported). {"type":"item.completed","item":{"id":"item_0","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc \"sed -n '1,260p' <TMPDIR>/plan.txt\"","aggregated_output":"## Plan\n\nFix two bugs from issue #6577: (1) false-positive policy-rejection kills when completed Codex command `aggregated_output` (including nested `item.aggregated_output`) quotes historical larch-log phrases; (2) attempt counters rendered as retries in execution-issue entries.\n\n### Approach\n\n**Primary fix — gated, recursive `aggregated_output` stripping before regex scan**\n\n`_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt` raw-scans the last 32 KB of the Codex events stream (JSONL from `codex exec --json`). Production events nest command output under `item.aggregated_output` in `item.completed` / `command_execution` rows (see `larch-logs/design/693522DE/execution-issues.md`). A flat top-level key strip misses the real false-positive path; stripping every `aggregated_output` would hide genuine rejections in failed commands.\n\nAdd `_sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan(text: str) -> str` (after the `_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt_bytes` constants block):\n\n1. Iterate lines of the bounded tail text.\n2. Non-JSON lines pass through unchanged (startup noise, legacy plain-text fixtures).\n3. JSON lines: `json.loads` each line; on `JSONDecodeError`, pass the original line through.\n4. Walk the parsed value recursively. For every dict that owns an `aggregated_output` key, gate removal on that dict's `exit_code`:\n   - **Strip** `aggregated_output` when `exit_code == 0` (successful completion whose stdout/stderr body can quote grep hits over committed larch-logs).\n   - **Strip** when `exit_code is None` and `aggregated_output` is empty (in-progress rows; no-op but safe).\n   - **Preserve** `aggregated_output` when `exit_code` is any other non-zero integer (failed command evidence must remain scannable).\n   - Recursion covers nested `item` dicts and any other dict nodes that carry both fields; do not assume a flat envelope.\n5. Re-serialize sanitized JSON objects with `json.dumps(..., ensure_ascii=False)` plus the original line terminator.\n\nIn `_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt`, call `_sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan(bounded)` to produce `scanned`, then use `scanned` everywhere `bounded` is used today (both regex guards, the `splitlines()` excerpt pass, and the fallback slice). Real policy rejections still surface via non-zero `exit_code` rows, error events, and plain-text startup/diag lines outside successful completions.\n\n**Secondary fix — retry suffix rendering**\n\n`_review_launcher.py` passes `auth_attempt` and `transient_attempt` starting at 1 (initial run). In `append_failure_main`, when both `--retry-count` and `--transient-retry-count` are present, subtract 1 from each before rendering; omit the retry suffix entirely when both adjusted values are 0 (single-attempt policy rejection). Leave the `elif args.retry_count:` ci-launcher branch unchanged (0-based counts).\n\n### Edge cases\n\n- Nested `item.completed` with `exit_code: 0` and trigger phrases only inside `item.aggregated_output` must not kill (primary regression target).\n- Nested or flat command row with non-zero `exit_code` and policy phrases in `aggregated_output` must still match after sanitization (preserves fast-kill for genuine failures).\n- `item.started` rows (`exit_code: null`, empty `aggregated_output`) pass through; stripping is a no-op.\n- Malformed JSON lines pass through unchanged; existing plain-text policy-rejection tests keep working.\n- Both adjusted retry counters at 0 suppress the suffix (desired for policy rejection with no retries).\n\n### Failure modes\n\n1. **`exit_code` absent on a dict with `aggregated_output`**: treat as preserve (do not strip) so ambiguous rows cannot hide real failures.\n2. **Deeply nested trees**: recursive walk must visit all dict nodes; shallow `item` pop alone is insufficient if Codex adds other nesting later.\n3. **Re-serialization changes key order**: irrelevant; regex scan is order-independent.\n\n### Testing strategy\n\n- Keep `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_rejection_fast_fails` unchanged (plain-text events still kill).\n- Keep `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_rejection_requires_both_families` unchanged.\n- Add `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_no_false_positive_aggregated_output`:\n  - Pre-write a realistic JSONL events file to `paths.events` before launch, shaped like production Codex output:\n    `{\"type\":\"item.completed\",\"item\":{\"id\":\"item_0\",\"type\":\"command_execution\",\"command\":\"/bin/bash -c \\\"rg ... larch-logs\\\"\",\"aggregated_output\":\"... exec_command failed ... Rejected(blocked by policy) ...\",\"exit_code\":0,\"status\":\"completed\"}}`\n  - Run a long-sleep stub child so the policy watcher has time to scan the events file.\n  - Assert the process is not killed early, diag lacks `FAILURE_CLASS=policy-rejection` and `POLICY_REJECTION=true`.\n- Add `test_sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan_preserves_failed_command_output` (unit-level, same module or test_agents):\n  - Feed a JSONL line with `exit_code: 1` and trigger phrases inside nested `item.aggregated_output`; assert sanitized text still contains the phrases.\n- Update `test_codex_transient_exhaustion_logs_to_implement_tmpdir`: change `\"transient-retries=5\"` to `\"transient-retries=4\"` (four retries after subtracting the initial attempt).\n\n### Files to modify/create\n\n### UPDATED: python/larch/agents/_run_external.py\n\n- Add `_should_strip_aggregated_output(exit_code: object) -> bool` returning true only for `exit_code == 0` or (`exit_code is None` and caller confirms empty output).\n- Add `_strip_gated_aggregated_output(node: object) -> None` recursive in-place walker: for each dict with `aggregated_output`, pop the key when `_should_strip_aggregated_output(node.get(\"exit_code\"))` is true; {"type":"item.completed","item":{"id":"item_0","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc \"sed -n '1,260p' <TMPDIR>/plan.txt\"","aggregated_output":"## Plan\n\nFix two bugs from issue #6577: (1) false-positive policy-rejection kills when completed Codex command `aggregated_output` (including nested `item.aggregated_output`) quotes historical larch-log phrases; (2) attempt counters rendered as retries in execution-issue entries.\n\n### Approach\n\n**Primary fix — gated, recursive `aggregated_output` stripping before regex scan**\n\n`_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt` raw-scans the last 32 KB of the Codex events stream (JSONL from `codex exec --json`). Production events nest command output under `item.aggregated_output` in `item.completed` / `command_execution` rows (see `larch-logs/design/693522DE/execution-issues.md`). A flat top-level key strip misses the real false-positive path; stripping every `aggregated_output` would hide genuine rejections in failed commands.\n\nAdd `_sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan(text: str) -> str` (after the `_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt_bytes` constants block):\n\n1. Iterate lines of the bounded tail text.\n2. Non-JSON lines pass through unchanged (startup noise, legacy plain-text fixtures).\n3. JSON lines: `json.loads` each line; on `JSONDecodeError`, pass the original line through.\n4. Walk the parsed value recursively. For every dict that owns an `aggregated_output` key, gate removal on that dict's `exit_code`:\n   - **Strip** `aggregated_output` when `exit_code == 0` (successful completion whose stdout/stderr body can quote grep hits over committed larch-logs).\n   - **Strip** when `exit_code is None` and `aggregated_output` is empty (in-progress rows; no-op but safe).\n   - **Preserve** `aggregated_output` when `exit_code` is any other non-zero integer (failed command evidence must remain scannable).\n   - Recursion covers nested `item` dicts and any other dict nodes that carry both fields; do not assume a flat envelope.\n5. Re-serialize sanitized JSON objects with `json.dumps(..., ensure_ascii=False)` plus the original line terminator.\n\nIn `_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt`, call `_sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan(bounded)` to produce `scanned`, then use `scanned` everywhere `bounded` is used today (both regex guards, the `splitlines()` excerpt pass, and the fallback slice). Real policy rejections still surface via non-zero `exit_code` rows, error events, and plain-text startup/diag lines outside successful completions.\n\n**Secondary fix — retry suffix rendering**\n\n`_review_launcher.py` passes `auth_attempt` and `transient_attempt` starting at 1 (initial run). In `append_failure_main`, when both `--retry-count` and `--transient-retry-count` are present, subtract 1 from each before rendering; omit the retry suffix entirely when both adjusted values are 0 (single-attempt policy rejection). Leave the `elif args.retry_count:` ci-launcher branch unchanged (0-based counts).\n\n### Edge cases\n\n- Nested `item.completed` with `exit_code: 0` and trigger phrases only inside `item.aggregated_output` must not kill (primary regression target).\n- Nested or flat command row with non-zero `exit_code` and policy phrases in `aggregated_output` must still match after sanitization (preserves fast-kill for genuine failures).\n- `item.started` rows (`exit_code: null`, empty `aggregated_output`) pass through; stripping is a no-op.\n- Malformed JSON lines pass through unchanged; existing plain-text policy-rejection tests keep working.\n- Both adjusted retry counters at 0 suppress the suffix (desired for policy rejection with no retries).\n\n### Failure modes\n\n1. **`exit_code` absent on a dict with `aggregated_output`**: treat as preserve (do not strip) so ambiguous rows cannot hide real failures.\n2. **Deeply nested trees**: recursive walk must visit all dict nodes; shallow `item` pop alone is insufficient if Codex adds other nesting later.\n3. **Re-serialization changes key order**: irrelevant; regex scan is order-independent.\n\n### Testing strategy\n\n- Keep `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_rejection_fast_fails` unchanged (plain-text events still kill).\n- Keep `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_rejection_requires_both_families` unchanged.\n- Add `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_no_false_positive_aggregated_output`:\n  - Pre-write a realistic JSONL events file to `paths.events` before launch, shaped like production Codex output:\n    `{\"type\":\"item.completed\",\"item\":{\"id\":\"item_0\",\"type\":\"command_execution\",\"command\":\"/bin/bash -c \\\"rg ... larch-logs\\\"\",\"aggregated_output\":\"... exec_command failed ... Rejected(blocked by policy) ...\",\"exit_code\":0,\"status\":\"completed\"}}`\n  - Run a long-sleep stub child so the policy watcher has time to scan the events file.\n  - Assert the process is not killed early, diag lacks `FAILURE_CLASS=policy-rejection` and `POLICY_REJECTION=true`.\n- Add `test_sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan_preserves_failed_command_output` (unit-level, same module or test_agents):\n  - Feed a JSONL line with `exit_code: 1` and trigger phrases inside nested `item.aggregated_output`; assert sanitized text still contains the phrases.\n- Update `test_codex_transient_exhaustion_logs_to_implement_tmpdir`: change `\"transient-retries=5\"` to `\"transient-retries=4\"` (four retries after subtracting the initial attempt).\n\n### Files to modify/create\n\n### UPDATED: python/larch/agents/_run_external.py\n\n- Add `_should_strip_aggregated_output(exit_code: object) -> bool` returning true only for `exit_code == 0` or (`exit_code is None` and caller confirms empty output).\n- Add `_strip_gated_aggregated_output(node: object) -> None` recursive in-place walker: for each dict with `aggregated_output`, pop the key when `_should_strip_aggregated_output(node.get(\"exit_code\"))` is true; recurse into dict values and list elements.\n- Add `_sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan(text: str) -> str`: line-wise JSON parse → `_strip_gated_aggregated_output` → re-serialize; non-JSON lines unchanged.\n- In `_codex_policy_rejection_excerpt`, replace direct use of `bounded` in regex/excerpt logic with `scanned = _sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan(bounded)`.\n\n### UPDATED: python/larch/report/run_logs.py\n\n- In `append_failure_main` (lines 654–657), when both retry counters are present: parse integers, subtract 1 from each, render `auth-retries` / `transient-retries` only when the adjusted value is > 0; skip the whole retry suffix when both adjusted values are 0.\n\n### UPDATED: python/tests/agents/test_agents.py\n\n- Add `test_run_external_agent_codex_policy_no_false_positive_aggregated_output` with nested `item.completed` / `command_execution` fixture (`exit_code: 0`) as above.\n- Add `test_sanitize_codex_events_for_policy_scan_preserves_failed_command_output` for the `exit_code != 0` preserve path.\n\n### UPDATED: python/tests/agents/test_launch_review.py\n\n- In `test_codex_transient_exhaustion_logs_to_implement_tmpdir`, change expected `\"transient-retries=5\"` to `\"transient-retries=4\"`.\n\ndifficulty: TRIVIAL\ndiff_lines: 95\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}
